## Turn 1 — USER REQUEST (Step 0)

> i work for a vdc consultancy.
you are an ai being used for estimation
typical coordination projects are 75-80% production and 20-5% admin work in terms of distribution of the budget.
admin is $150/hr and production is $85/hr
coordination is split into 3 levels and then there are add alternates
level 1 coordination is setting up the project, existing conditions lidar scanning and modeling, if applicable, proposed modeling, and then setting the acc environment for coordination. we run the first clash to show project status before coordination and give a report. there is always a matterport at the beginning at $1500 a visit. lidar scans are also $1500 a visit. aside from that we do a review of the drawings for constructability, feasibility, and find info that is missing, contradictory, or wrong. this is also put in a report. if there are existing pdfs or cad drawings, we overlay that with the existing scan in what we call a deviation study to show the likely discrepancies and worth of the scan vs using reference drawings. we also align the project to global coordinates with the use of survey control and civil drawings, if required. in terms of modelling, we do all trades, arch, struct, and mepf. usual exclusions are modeling rebar, trees and shrubs. the rest of the landscape and hardscape is modeled. low voltage and av work is also included. solar if necessary. pools are usually modeled for hold-to's in that we do not detail finishes but model the equipment for coordination and the hole for the pool excavation just to provide hold-to dimensions. things are modeled to lod 300-350. we do model stud wall framing. admin sets up the revit templates, acc, does qc, hosts 30min daily meetings with the production team, and then meetings as needed with the client for more info. they also process and align all point clouds. as well the reference sheet set is replicated so that the final coordinated model has the same setup as the reference drawings. effort is published in acc daily for other stakeholders with no
<truncated 723 bytes>
ions and annotations as necessary, as things are no longer moving. level 3 coordination is record modeling. this is when we lidar scan for deviations in what is built vs what was proposed. deviations that do not queue change orders or rework and are kept are scanned and then the model and sheets updated to reflect what is built not what is proposed. that way if there are future changes to the site a new lidar scan is not needed because the drawings are reliable. we usually reserve $20k flat rate for this. this is reflected as an add alt. based on the timeline of the project we say that there is a matterport scan 2x a month for the duration of construction at $1500 a scan. this is reflected as an add alt. any studies such as a solar study is also an add alternate. pricing is $3000 flat rate. any other additions are add alternates. pricing should be broke down by level of coordination and then by production and admin since there are 2 different rates. the add alternates are just a flat rate and don't need to be broken down by role. when it comes to estimating you should ask for the trades being considered, timeline, and any add alternates, and should be able to prepare a proposal from that. keep in mind the speed of modeling is usually 2500-3500sqft/week all trades included scan rate reflects day rate and then there's a day for processing before it gets into the project. all projects are done in revit, version doesn't make a difference. references come in the form of pdfs, cad, and/or revit files. sometimes i may supplement with drawings just to make things easier in terms of scale and scope. so consider those as well."
